- **Whole spec, row 13 and row 11** — Inherited from Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (Settled): row 13, the hidden edge, is held back in every Chapter (Aeries and Aalia show no more than their level; the fighters of the early raid carry no hidden edge); row 11 is planted a second time in this Chapter (answered 2026-10-05 and 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, thread returns** — Inherited from Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1: The Lone Banner is left open on purpose and no Chapter of this part answers it; Gale holds two reports and the same question; Messages Between the Kingdoms returns in Chapter 005 as Gale's end by absence and in Chapter 009 as met again (answered 2026-10-05, 2026-10-08 and 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, the king off the page** — Inherited from Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (Settled): King Osgar Brack is registered as a villain, stays off the page as a person for all of Section 1 and is spoken of; he is named in his raiders' thoughts and never shown (answered 2026-10-08; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, the Chapter 006 village link** — Inherited from Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 and Section Spec 1.1 (v1.4, Skipped and Settled): the reader first sees the link between Brackenhold and the village's hostile neighbour in Chapter 006, from something Bison sees; this Chapter keeps its Gale mine road clear of the village's border (answered 2026-10-08 and 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18).

**Inherited from Section Spec 1.1, through Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (Settled items that bear on this Chapter)**
- **Whole spec, the world** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1: not a tour; the world and its lore are built in as living texture and met in use (answered 2026-10-02; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03 and 04, technology** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1: technology is shown in deeds, woven in and led openly by Valia; it is not second fiddle and not hidden through Sections 1 to 8 (answered 2026-10-02 and 2026-10-05; carried 2026-10-18). In this Chapter it is Gale's end of the *Hourline* planted by absence; nothing is explained.
- **Whole spec, the alliance** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1: this part carries the alliance's warmth only; the first exchange between the courts is in Novel-Part 2 and none is planned here; there is no standing dispute between the houses, and the strain comes only from outside pressure (answered 2026-10-02; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, no open war** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1 (Locked Canon): no kingdom declares open war; the Section's own raids begin in Novel-Part 3; no strike falls on Azorion; the raids are not the alliance's first test, which begins in Novel-Part 3 (answered 2026-10-02 and 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, ages** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1: Varya's birthday is Year 1247, Arvelle, Day 6 (Phase Day 98) and Aegon's is Year 1247, Sorvelle, Day 10 (Phase Day 132), both after this part; the children are eight throughout (answered 2026-10-04; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Header, Threads Carried Out** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1: no figure is set for any thread's return; every return note reads unset (answered 2026-10-02; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, Carried-by lines** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1: each Carried-by line is a loose guide the Novel-Part Spec may change freely when it decides who is on the page (answered 2026-10-02; carried 2026-10-18).
- **Area 03, Highgale** — Inherited from Section Spec 1.1 (v1.4, Name): Highgale is the author's own name for the Gale capital city on the plateau floor (ruled 2026-10-18); it is a working label above Chapter grain (answered 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18).

**Deferred (carried from higher specs and from the ledgers)**
- **Whole spec, the names of the escort, the districts and the techniques** — Deferred: the names of the captain and the five warriors, the districts of Highgale and the names of Aeries's and Aalia's techniques fell due at Chapter Spec 005. Deferred by the author to: Chapter Spec 005 (Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1, 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18). Answered in this build: the seven escort names and the eight district names are the author's (Settled); the two techniques are the planner's picks from the kit moves, flagged (Skipped).
- **Area 01, the Gale mine's name** — Deferred: the mine's name stood as a placeholder. Deferred by the author to: Chapter Spec 005 (Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1, 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18). Answered in this build: Whistledeep, the author's pick (Settled).
- **Whole spec, Gale address-form holds and Dual-POV marks** — Deferred: address forms the Gale ledgers leave open and any Dual-POV mark are the Chapter Spec's and the author's (Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1, 2026-10-05; carried 2026-10-18). Answered in this build: the address ruling (Settled); no Area is marked Dual-POV.
- **Header and Protagonist Thread Map, the five arc cells of Aeries and Aalia** — Deferred: Stated Want, Underlying Need, Deepest Fear, Belief That Blocks the Need and the Section 1.5 arc vector cell fell due at Chapter Spec 005. Deferred by the author to: Chapter Spec 005 (Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1, 2026-10-05; carried 2026-10-18). Asked and settled in this build (Settled).
- **Areas 05 and 06, how and by whom Gale is told** — Deferred: how and by whom Gale is told of the raid from before Day One, and whether it shows on the page. Deferred by the author to: Chapter Spec 005 (Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1, 2026-10-08; carried 2026-10-18). Answered in part by the author's rulings of this build (Settled): Varys tells by his own letter, it shows on the page, a Gale courier-flight pilot carries it from the corridor post with the post-master's word behind it, and Varys writes on his own weighing.
- **Whole spec, when the Azure Sky court and Gale learn that the raiders were Brackenhold's** — Deferred: when and how either court learns whose raiders struck the two caravans. Deferred by the author to: the author, at a later spec, and definitely not in Novel-Part 1 (Section Spec 1.1 and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1, 2026-10-08, widened to both courts and both early raids on 2026-10-18; carried 2026-10-18). Nothing in this Chapter touches it.
- **Areas 02, 05 and 06, how the raiders found the two caravans** — Deferred: the question of how the company found a mine caravan in Azure Sky before Day One and a mine caravan in Gale on Phase Day 9 stays open. Deferred by the author to: Novel-Part 3, at its build (the author's ruling of 2026-10-18: "left open to Novel-Part 3"). Neither the reader nor Gale is shown how in this Chapter. Gale holds one clue to it, the clamp that Varys's letter tells as a plain confirmed fact, and cannot test it at its own end of the wire (Settled, below); no source, band or kingdom is named.
- **Whole spec, the rivals' aims and rulers** — Deferred: the aims and rulers of Cindervast, Ironshore and Saltmere. Deferred by the author to: the Novel-Part Spec of Novel-Part 3, at its build (Section Spec 1.1, 2026-10-02; carried 2026-10-18). Not touched by this Chapter.
- **Whole spec, the village's land** — Deferred: which kingdom's land the border village lies in. Deferred by the author to: the Chapter Spec of each Chapter the village touches, or the Area (Section Spec 1.1, 2026-10-02; carried 2026-10-18). This Chapter does not touch the village.

**Settled at this build (the author's rulings and answers of 2026-10-18, relayed by the orchestrating command)**
- **Header, Build mode, Chapter pacing and Chapter size target** — Settled: the Chapter is built whole; the Chapter pacing is Medium; the Chapter has six Area blocks, each with a size target of 3,000 words and each written at 72 percent (about 2,160 words); the Chapter word budget is 18,000 (about 12,960 words as drafted) (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Every Area, Budget stretch** — Settled: the stretch is up to 0.72 of target on all six Area blocks, for these reasons: Areas 01 and 02, a fight and the fates of the escort; Areas 03 and 04, a world debut and a named technique in each; Areas 05 and 06, two letters, a messenger and an open question (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Header, title** — Settled: the title "The House With No Walls" is kept (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03, 05 and 06, the Court** — Settled: the Court is a descriptor, held on the open terrace of the Aerie Palace, with no named hall (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Section 5.0, the Scene Map** — Settled: S13 is Areas 01 and 02 (Fast, Day 9); S14 is Areas 03 and 04 (Slow, Day 12); S15 is Areas 05 and 06 (Medium, Day 14); a visible break shows before each scene and before each change of viewpoint; each scene carries its date line (Year 1247, Ilovany, Nuvil, Day 9; Alovil, Day 12; Mavil, Day 14); the Chapter window runs from Day 9 (Phase Day 9) to Day 14 (Phase Day 14); no scene crosses a Chapter seam (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the raid, who and when** — Settled: the raid on a Gale mine caravan is by the Redhaft Vanguard of Brackenhold, led by Wulfric Rask (a Tier B villain), and it is reader-only that they are Brackenhold's; the company is twenty and more raiders in plain clothes, with the same sign and the same count as in Chapter 003, so that the reader knows them again; the caravan comes from the mine Whistledeep (the author's pick) with Power Crystal, in bulk and of low grade, for the Aerie Artificers' College; the raid falls at first light with ground mist, at the foot of the Windward Range on Gale's northern frontier with Brackenhold; the company takes the ore and the manifest (the route papers); the reader gets both repeat clues, Rask's sign and count and a glimpse of the man in the good coat at the edge, an echo of Chapter 003's informant (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the escort** — Settled: the escort is all Tier C Gale mine-escort warriors, and none survives: Sigrun Korth (female, Warrior, Level 7 High, a Captain of the Gale Windward Host riding the escort in person); Torvek Gant (male, Level 4 High); Yrsa Wold (female, Level 4 Mid); Jorund Ulm (male, Level 4 Low); Ingrith Mott (female, Level 3 Peak); Egil Hurn (male, Level 3 Mid); the traits: Sigrun Korth recites standing orders at every halt, Torvek Gant palms each wheel hub, Yrsa Wold draws the road in dust before speaking, Jorund Ulm never leaves the strongbox bench, Ingrith Mott talks to the mules, Egil Hurn laughs short and early (answered 2026-10-18). The tier of these six, Tier C in this answer, is replaced by the author's later ruling, the Settled note on the tier of the Gale people below.
- **Areas 01 and 02, viewpoints** — Settled: Area 01 is in the viewpoint of Sigrun Korth, who needs Layers L02 and L07 registered; Area 02 is in the viewpoint of Wulfric Rask, who needs the Tier B registration and Layers L02 and L07 (answered 2026-10-18; Rask's are met by the ledger step of the same day, Audit). The registration asked of Sigrun Korth is replaced by the author's later ruling that she is a Tier D viewpoint holder (below).
- **Area 02, the Vanguard's conduct** — Settled: the Vanguard has no standing rule against killing soldiers (the author lifted the order of Chapter 003, which stays as written) (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, Part 44** — Settled: each fight Area carries an Engagement Ledger; the captain stands Shut against Rask; the offsets are named; no gap arithmetic reaches the prose; the information source for the ambush does not touch the author-held Brackenhold matter (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 05 and 06, what Gale people know** — Settled: the Gale people on the page know only plain-clothes, unknown raiders; Rask may be named only by the narration in his own viewpoint and never by Gale people (answered 2026-10-18). The ground-reading ruling of the same day (below) adds what the patrol reads from the ground to the first clause.
- **Areas 01 and 02, the place of the raid** — Settled: the border placement is the foot of the Windward Range, on Gale's northern frontier, and needs a ledger map ruling and a Brackenhold ledger update with the Gale frontier at the Gate 2; the author asked for a fuller Brackenhold ledger file (answered 2026-10-18). The road, ford, pass and stair names are the planner's picks from the author's cleared set (Skipped).
- **Areas 03 and 04, the day in Gale** — Settled: Area 03 opens with Aeries reading the wind, an opening sweep through his senses that carries the plateau, Highgale (the capital, named in the ledger) and its districts Frame Row, Rack Market, Courier Green, Muster Field, Leeside, Weighhouse Row, Elders' Rise and Ropewalk (all eight accepted by the author, each with a one-clause picture on first use), then the man at the palace's open edge; the Court's stones are named the Stone Ring (the author's own name); Aeries shows one named technique in Area 03 and Aalia one in Area 04, at her dusk ward-walk, each taken as a kit move by its exact ledger name from their ledger kits (Aeries `MAN-SIG-GRS-007`, `-009` and `-010`; Aalia `MAN-SIG-SHS-009`, `MAN-SIG-BFS-009`, `MAN-SIG-HKS-008` and `MAN-SIG-DAG-010`); the techniques are shown once and never explained; the hidden edge of row 13 is held back, and adults show no more than their level (answered 2026-10-18). The two picks are the planner's (Skipped).
- **Areas 03 and 04, Aegon's letter** — Settled: a letter from Aegon, written on the evening of Phase Day 8 in Azorion, arrives in Area 03 or Area 04; the author wants it; the exclusion of Chapters 003 and 004 on a letter from Aegon to Gale is lifted for this Chapter; Aegon's heritage is neither named nor hinted; he writes as a child of eight; Valia's Day 2 note to Aalia is answered by effect and not shown; Gale's end of the *Hourline* is planted by absence, in a line about couriers where the wire stops (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, the dropped items** — Settled: the open-ring promise, courier-flights as a scene, and Aeries's night office and night flight are dropped from this Chapter (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Areas 05 and 06, the news** — Settled: Varys's handwritten letter is his own weighing, with no new event, confirmed facts only, no guess, no source named and no kingdom named; it is carried by a Gale courier-flight pilot from the corridor post with the post-master's word standing behind it, and it is read on the open terrace; Aeries reacts, rigid and silent for one breath; then the patrol officer Dagmar Sund (female, of the Windward Host's Ridgewatch detachment; Tier C in this answer and Tier D by the author's later ruling below) reports the Day 9 raid and the empty caravan in her own words as an ordinary count, and the finding of the caravan is reported only and never shown; the lingering question is how the raiders found two caravans in two kingdoms, left open to Novel-Part 3; Aeries writes to Varys and tells him of the Day 9 raid, binding nothing (the Pause precedes any commitment; no war is declared, no joint action scheduled and no formal exchange begun); Aalia writes to Valia, answering the note and the news; the Chapter ends on the letters written and sealed; both courts hold both raids at the close, with no source named (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Area 05, the five days** — Settled: the lag from Day 9 to Day 14 gets a reason in the plan: the seasonal Windward Trail has only just opened, the Kite Line does not reach the mine, and the patrol finds the caravan later (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, Chapter 006 and Chapter 003** — Settled: the Chapter 006 village link is unchanged and the Whistledeep road is kept clear of the village's border, with no Moon-Sect words and no word of the ground north of the Range; Chapter 003 stays as written (answered 2026-10-18).
- **Protagonist Thread Map, Aeries's five arc cells** — Settled: Stated Want: that his son has a childhood in which the sky is not a duty. Underlying Need: to be known whole by more than one person. Deepest Fear: that his son inherits the same cage. Belief That Blocks the Need: "What is held must never be set down where people can see." Part 30 Section 1.5 Arc Vector: Part 30 §1.5, the Aeries arc vector ("The Dragon-King and the Weight of Blood"), as written and cited by name, read through the Section 1 slice (answered 2026-10-18). The cells are ledger text, written to `aeries` Section 9 at the one Gate 2; the spec holds the answers and the ledger holds the facts, and the spec fixes no further wording.
- **Protagonist Thread Map, Aalia's five arc cells** — Settled: Stated Want: to see her son grow into himself. Underlying Need: to be understood without having to explain. Deepest Fear: losing the few she has let in. Belief That Blocks the Need: "What I cannot explain I cannot share." Part 30 Section 1.5 Arc Vector: Part 30 §1.5, the Aalia arc vector ("The Dragon-Queen and the Heart of the Alliance"), as written and cited by name, read through the Section 1 slice (answered 2026-10-18). The cells are ledger text, written to `aalia` Section 9 at the one Gate 2; the spec holds the answers and the ledger holds the facts.
- **Whole spec, address of Gale people (supersedes ledger text)** — Settled: Gale people say "Sire" and "Madam" in a private setting and "Your Majesty" in a public setting; "Wind-Crown" and "Storm-Counsel" are not used; keep it simple; no wind-names; birth names; ranks as bare rank nouns; the Gale acknowledgment words are therefore not used (the author's ruling of 2026-10-18, which supersedes the ledger text, `aeries` Section 7, `aalia` Section 7, `gale-kingdom-state` §G16 and `aerie-palace` Section 3; the ledger step is flagged for the Gate 2 in the Open notes).
- **Whole spec, Varys writes on his own weighing** — Settled: Varys writes on his own weighing, and his letter is not a reply to any event (answered 2026-10-18). How this meets his line of Chapter 003, "when I have something to tell him that is true, and not before", is the planner's reading, in the Skipped notes.
- **Areas 05 and 06, Dagmar Sund is a Lieutenant** — Settled: Dagmar Sund is a Lieutenant of the Windward Host, not a Sergeant; a Lieutenant commands a platoon (`gale-kingdom-military` section 6.1), and the ledger assigns no realm to any post, so her Senior Level 9 High is her own; the pilots of the courier-flights stay a Senior of the lower Levels, as set (answered 2026-10-18, relayed after the first draft). The planner's earlier pick of the rank noun Sergeant is withdrawn.
- **Whole spec, the plan levels** — Settled: the levels set in the plan are confirmed as drafted: the unnamed squad leader at Warrior Level 7 Low (the other squad leader is Hadric Stoll, at Warrior Level 6 with the stage left open, see below), the three ranks of line fighters (Warrior Level 5 Low to Mid, Level 4 Mid to High, and Level 3 High to Level 4 Low), Dagmar Sund at Senior Level 9 High and the pilots at a Senior of the lower Levels, beside the seven levels the author gave (answered 2026-10-18, relayed after the first draft). The company's count and its split into three ranks stay the planner's picks (Skipped), and the final sweep re-lists them.
- **Areas 01, 02, 05 and 06, no one of the Gale caravan lives** — Settled: no one survives: the four carters and the clerk die with the escort, none is left for dead and none is found alive; the author's first answer was "a few carters, all killed, unnamed" and this ruling keeps it (answered 2026-10-18, relayed after the first draft). The planner's earlier pick of a hurt carter found alive under the third wagon is withdrawn. What the Gale people know, plain clothes and unknown raiders, therefore comes from what the patrol reads on the ground and from no witness; the mechanism is the planner's proposal (Skipped), and the question of how the raiders found the road stays open.
- **Areas 02, 05 and 06, the Captain's company loses its standing orders** — Settled: Sigrun Korth's death costs her company its standing orders (`gale-kingdom-military` section 6.8, where a commander holds her formation's standing orders personally and a successor does not inherit them); this is told as one plain line in Dagmar Sund's count, which the king hears and does not answer, and the matter is left open: the Chapter does not say who gives the company new orders or when (answered 2026-10-18, relayed after the first draft). The spec holds no ledger value; the line is ledger text written at the Gate 2.
- **Areas 03 and 04, Aeries's technique at dusk** — Settled: Aeries shows ***Vigorrondel*** at dusk on the open edge of the terrace, as planned (answered 2026-10-18, relayed after the first draft). This departs once from the late-night session his ledger registers (`B39-L24-SESSION-05`; the outdoor place of `B39-L24-PLACE-03` is kept) by the author's ruling; the Gate 2 marks it in his Section 10 row as the author's ruled variation, and the Gate 1 continuity-checker reads it under Check #21 as information.
- **Areas 05 and 06, Varys's letter carries the wire clamp** — Settled: Varys's letter includes the clamp found on the wire (Chapter 003) as one plain confirmed fact: a clamp was found on the post's wire at the relay hut by the lower ford, and the post's first dispatch was lost to it, which is why the Court heard late (answered 2026-10-18, relayed after the first draft). The author overrode the planner's recommendation to leave it out. Gale therefore holds one clue to the leak and cannot test it, because Gale's own end of the wire is absent; no source, no band and no kingdom is named by any character; the letter draws no inference from the clamp and tells none of the Court's guesses or the measures taken; the king's question, how the raiders found the road and the day, stays open (the Deferred note to Novel-Part 3 is unchanged).
- **Areas 01 to 06, the collective codes** — Settled: the Aerie Palace takes a short collective code and the mine escort takes a short collective code; Highgale and the Ridgewatch patrol take none, by the author's ruling (answered 2026-10-18, in the audit round, as relayed by the orchestrating command). The wording of the two short codes was confirmed by the author the same day and stands in the Settled notes that follow; the two rulings of none need no block and no ledger write. For Stirrup Ford and the Seam Road, none is the planner's reading and not the author's ruling (see the Skipped note on the collective code of the ford and the road). This replaces the planner's earlier recommendation of no code for the mine escort.
- **Areas 01, 02, 05 and 06, the tier of the Gale people** — Settled: the seven named Gale people (Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott, Egil Hurn and Dagmar Sund) are Tier D, not Tier C, with their names, levels, traits and moves as set. A Tier D person is a registry row with a code picked at the writing, may speak freely and carries no Layer registration (Part 39 Rule 39.4.3.1a; Part 19), so no registration work is owed before drafting. The author ruled this over the reading that a person a Chapter Spec names is never Tier D, in these words: "Change gale tier C to all Tier D and not immediate work left" (answered 2026-10-18, in chat). Sigrun Korth is the viewpoint of Area 01 as a Tier D viewpoint holder: Layers L02 and L07 are not registered for her, and the Gate 1 reads that as information. The deaths and consequences of Tier D persons go to the author under Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4, and the author ruled them as planned: all six of the escort and all five hands die. Any later promotion of a Gale person to a higher tier is the author's. Hadric Stoll stays Tier C (already registered) and Wulfric Rask stays a Tier B villain. This replaces the Tier C reading in the earlier Settled notes on the escort, on the viewpoints and on Dagmar Sund's report.
- **Areas 01 and 02, the information behind the ambush** — Settled: the Engagement Ledgers name the kind of information source without choosing one: a leak or a scout's report of the route and the hour, and which of the two it was is the author's open question, left to Novel-Part 3; the time is the days since the Windward Trail opened to horses; the place is the ford, the draw and the far bank (answered 2026-10-18, in chat).
- **Areas 05 and 06, what Gale learns from the ground** — Settled: the ground-reading is kept: the patrol reads plain coats, long hafts, a company of about two dozen, a mule-track and that the raiders carried their own dead out, with no source, no band and no kingdom named (answered 2026-10-18, in chat). It goes beyond the words "plain clothes and no more" of Section Spec 1.1 (blocks 7 and 8) and of the Novel-Part Spec entry, so a Variance note (information) records the difference.
- **Areas 02 and 05, the raiders' track** — Settled: the track goes up the draw and is lost on the shale, and Shalegate Pass is not on the track; the exclusion of the ground north of the Range is kept in plain words, with the far country not named (answered 2026-10-18, in chat).
- **Areas 01 and 02, Hadric Stoll rides on Day 9** — Settled: Hadric Stoll (Tier C side villain, Warrior Level 6) rides with the company as one of its two squad leaders and is a named acting person in Area 02; the other squad leader stays unnamed at Warrior Level 7 Low, and no other raider is named (answered 2026-10-18, in chat). His registration and the Threshold C-2 note are in the Audit notes, and the stage of his Level 6 is unset (the next note, and Open).
- **Areas 01 and 02, who leads the Vanguard on the field, and the stage of Stoll's Level 6** — Settled: Wulfric Rask leads the Redhaft Vanguard on the field, and Hadric Stoll rides as the Warrior Level 6 squad leader; the stage of Stoll's Level 6 is unset, since the author's answer on it was only that Rask is there as the Vanguard's leader, and no stage is set by implication (answered 2026-10-18, in chat). The Engagement Ledgers use the step range only, and the Open note on the stage stays open for the author.
- **Area 02, who ends the captain** — Settled: Wulfric Rask ends the captain himself, by ***Subjugating Hookback*** and his halberd (answered 2026-10-18, in chat). His kill state is registered (`B39-L25-KILL-02`) and his costly behavior fits it (`B39-L36-COST-10`, he ends every raid himself, in the open); `B39-L25-MERCY-08` (derived: mercy is delegated) is an information item for the Gate 1 checker and not a question for the author.
- **Areas 03 to 06, the Aerie Palace's Collective Behavior Code (Part 39 Rule 39.23.1.5)** — Settled: the Aerie Palace, a place where two or more named people act together (Aeries, Aalia, Dagmar Sund and the Court's people), takes a short code named THE OPEN EDGE, with the wording the author confirmed on 2026-10-18: "(1) no wall and no waiting room, (2) said standing, in the open, the hearers are the record". Built from: `aerie-palace` sections 1 and 5.1 and `aeries` `B39-L13-DOWN-99` (the first provision), and `aerie-palace` section 5.2 and `gale-kingdom-state`, the code's element SPOKEN INTO THE WIND (the second). Its home is `aerie-palace`. The code was not yet registered when this spec was built (`aeries` Section 11.5 records the precinct's location code as pending), and the Gale ruling-state code is registered and needs nothing more here. The ledger lane writes the block before drafting; this spec writes no ledger block and holds no ledger value (Rule 1.3.2), and a drafting agent never invents a code. The Behavioral Plan lines of Areas 03 to 06 name the code and describe its two provisions at more length; the confirmed wording above, and the ledger block written from it, govern. This note replaces the blocking Open note of the first draft, which proposed the code (answered 2026-10-18, confirmed by the author and relayed by the orchestrating command).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the mine escort's Collective Behavior Code (Part 39 Rule 39.23.1.5)** — Settled: the captain and her five (Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott and Egil Hurn) act together in Areas 01 and 02 and take a short code named THE RECITAL, with the wording the author confirmed on 2026-10-18: "(1) SAID AT EVERY HALT: the captain speaks the standing orders aloud, nothing written; (2) THE ORDERS LIVE IN THE CAPTAIN: her fall takes the orders with her". Its home is `gale-kingdom-military` (the author's confirmation), the file that holds the Windward Host's units. Built from: `gale-kingdom-state`, the Collective Behavior Code element SPOKEN INTO THE WIND, as `gale-kingdom-military` section 6.8 quotes it; `gale-kingdom-military` section 6.8, points 5 and 9 and items (a) and (b); the trait the author gave Sigrun Korth on 2026-10-18 (she recites standing orders at every halt); and the author's ruling of 2026-10-18 that her death costs her company its standing orders (Settled above). The six individual traits the author gave stay individual registrations and are not restated in the code. The code was not yet registered when this spec was built. The ledger lane writes the block before drafting; this spec writes no ledger block and holds no ledger value (Rule 1.3.2), and a drafting agent never invents a code. The Behavioral Plan lines of Areas 01 and 02 name the code and describe its two provisions at more length; the confirmed wording above, and the ledger block written from it, govern. The Redhaft Vanguard and the Gale ruling state are registered and need nothing. This note replaces the blocking Open note of the first draft, which proposed the code (answered 2026-10-18, confirmed by the author and relayed by the orchestrating command).
- **Whole spec, the departures from the Novel-Part Spec entry (the Variance group)** — Settled: at the confirm step the author was asked whether to amend the Novel-Part Spec entry, and Section Spec 1.1 where the departures touch it, or to keep the original as the plan baseline. The author answered: leave the entry as the plan baseline, with variance notes only (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step). No plan text of Section Spec 1.1 or Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 changes. The traced variance notes, one for each of six departures (the tier of the Gale people, Hadric Stoll as a squad leader, the ground-reading, Dagmar Sund as a Lieutenant, Aegon's letter and the clamp in Varys's letter), were appended on 2026-10-18, top-down in one step, to Section Spec 1.1 first and then to Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (Part 38 Rules 9.4.3 and 9.4.5). The Variance notes below that ask "the author's answer on whether to amend" now carry this answer.
- **Area 01, Sigrun Korth as a Tier D viewpoint holder (the answer at the confirm step)** — Settled: Sigrun Korth holds the viewpoint of Area 01 as a Tier D person with no registered Layers L02 and L07, by the author's ruling on the tier of the Gale people (the Settled note above), and the Gate 1 reads that as information. Her perception and thought come from the sources the Behavioral Plan of Area 01 names, and the drafting agent does not fill the gap by borrowing another character's way of seeing (Part 36 Rule 18.2.7). Asked at the confirm step about this viewpoint, the author answered: "None; proceed as ruled" (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step).

**Skipped (the planner's choices on items the author left to the planner; each settles nothing, and the final sweep re-lists them for the author's last chance)**
- **Areas 01, 02 and 05, the places** — Skipped: planner chose to assign the author's cleared names as follows: the raid falls at Stirrup Ford, where the Seam Road, the mine road from Whistledeep, crosses a stream on a gravel bar at the foot of the Range; Shalegate Pass is the high pass on the Range above the ford, named in passing and tied to no track (the raiders' track goes up the draw and is lost on the shale, and nothing beyond is shown); the Mule Stair is the cut-step stair that takes the road down from the ford toward the plateau's north edge and the Ridgewatch post. Other readings of the four names are lawful; the author may swap any (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the company's strength, ranks and levels** — Skipped: planner chose twenty-four in all (Rask, two squad leaders and twenty-one line fighters in three ranks: six at Warrior Level 5 Low to Mid, nine at Level 4 Mid to High, six at Level 3 High to Level 4 Low), the squad leaders Hadric Stoll at Warrior Level 6 (the stage not set) and one unnamed at Warrior Level 7 Low, and a rear rank that crosses upstream and a pack-line that waits in the draw above the ford. The author gave "twenty and more raiders", Rask's level and Chapter 003's bands (line fighters Level 3 to 5, squad leaders Level 6 to 7). The levels are confirmed by the author (Settled); the count and the three ranks are the planner's picks, re-listed at the sweep (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the escort's weapons and moves** — Skipped: planner chose a saber for all six, the Windward Host's Common stock. Each of the six first shows one Basic saber guard move, all from Common Basic manuals whose Gale rows are open library stock (`kingdoms/gale.csv`, access OPEN), so that the links of the Intermediate manuals count as met (Part 42 eligibility E3): the captain's ***Hooding Backedge*** (MV-BAS-SAB-005-01), Torvek Gant's ***Tight Basket*** (MV-BAS-SAB-008-03), Yrsa Wold's ***Ready Backedge*** (MV-BAS-SAB-011-03), Jorund Ulm's ***Swell Cowl*** (MV-BAS-SAB-002-04), Ingrith Mott's ***Resting Belly*** (MV-BAS-SAB-005-03) and Egil Hurn's ***Yelmancowl*** (MV-BAS-SAB-011-05). Then come the captain's Intermediate moves ***Basketscreen*** (MV-INT-SAB-003-05) and ***Swipe Swat*** (MV-INT-SAB-007-05) and the five warriors' Basic-type moves ***Swellbarrier*** (MV-INT-SAB-003-03), ***Falsedge Barrier*** (MV-INT-SAB-003-04), ***Yelmanprop*** (MV-INT-SAB-007-03), ***Whirlflog*** (MV-INT-SAB-007-01) and ***Rushing Gallop*** (MV-INT-SAB-007-02), all from `MAN-INT-SAB-003` (*Handbook of Guard and Riposte*) and `MAN-INT-SAB-007` (the *Wheeling Saber Canon*), both lent by the Windward Host. All of these are new, first used here. The page shows the manuals by their movements and never by a book. The raiders' moves are the Vanguard's Brackenhold halberd moves and the Common halberd moves it lends: ***Brawnhaft***, ***Rushing Axeblade***, ***Levering Hookpull***, ***Rubble Axefall***, ***Staticsnatch***, ***Subjugating Hookback*** and ***Axefall Decree*** are reused from Chapter 003 (***Brawnhaft*** and ***Staticsnatch*** are Hadric Stoll's registered moves), and ***Keen Axeblade***, ***Cinderheave***, ***Stalling Haft*** and ***Stalwart Socketring*** are new. Eligibility (E1 to E8) is read at Gate 1 (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the order in which the five hands fall** — Skipped: planner chose that the lead carter and one more are killed in the first rush, the clerk and a third carter in the mist as the file breaks, and the last carter, hiding under the third wagon, is found and killed by the rear rank in Area 02, so that Rask's count of the hands closes at five and none left; the killing of the hands is not drawn. The author's ruling is that all five die (Settled); the order is the planner's, and any other order with five dead is lawful (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01, 02, 05 and 06, how Gale reads plain coats and unknown raiders with no witness** — Skipped: planner chose a ground-reading as the only source of what Gale knows of the raiders, kept by the author's ruling (Settled) and told in the lieutenant's count of Area 05; only two of its items are shown on the page as traces, both in Area 01, and Area 02 shows the events that leave the rest and never a trace, in five items: (1) the sacking torn off the long hafts in one pull at the sign and dropped where it falls, most of it gathered as the company goes and the strips the mist hid left on the thorn and in the shingle (shown in Area 01 anchor 4, where ***Brawnhaft*** lets the wrap fall behind; the code's NO MARK holds that the hafts stay wrapped in sacking until the sign and that the sacking comes off in one pull, `redhaft-vanguard`); (2) the round butt-marks of the hafts stood in a line in the soft ground at the trees' edge on both banks, some two dozen (the result of the company's wait in Area 02 anchor 1, not shown as a trace; the main body waits on the near bank and the rear rank on the far bank); (3) a torn strip of plain undyed cloth on the third wagon's tailboard and one plain brown coat left in the shallows by the lead wheel, after one of the captain's cuts threw a line fighter into the water and he was hauled out of his coat and another's sleeve was left hanging (shown in Area 01 anchor 5; the code's coats are "brown and grey and undyed wool", bought at three markets, with no badge, cord, colour or word); (4) about two dozen boots and the track of a train of pack-mules going up the draw above the ford and lost on the shale (the mule-train's going in Area 02 anchor 7a, not shown as a trace); (5) blood on the shingle that is not Gale's and a dark line of it going up the draw, with none of the company's dead left behind (the fallen carried out in Area 02 anchor 7a, not shown as a trace). Each trace says what the company wore and carried and says nothing of whose it was: the code covers the mark and the name, and the ground keeps the rest. Rask's Operational Belief, "nothing is left on the road" (`wulfric-rask` Section 4), is met by the company's clearing of everything it can see (its fallen, its gear and most of its sacking); the traces are the ground itself (boots, butt-marks, the mule-track, the blood) and the few small things that the mist and the fight hid from the clearing (a strip of sacking on a thorn, a strip of cloth, a coat the water took), none of which Rask's viewpoint shows. The lieutenant says this in plain words, which keeps the "unknown" and the question open. Other lawful sources: a torn strip of cloth alone, or the wounds of the dead; both give less, and neither gives long hafts. Recommended: the five traces, because together they give plain coats, long hafts and about two dozen with no witness. The Gate 1 checker reads the traces against the code (NO MARK, THE LINE IS QUIET) and against the author-held matter (nothing in them touches it), and reads the Area 05 report against the Engagement Ledgers (Part 44 Rule 44.9.2.1) (2026-10-18).
- **Area 02, the take** — Skipped: planner chose that the company takes the ore on its own pack-mules, waiting in the draw above the ford (preparation), because the cargo is bulk, and takes the manifest from the strongbox; nothing that marks the company is left behind and its five dead are carried out; the sacking, the butt-marks, the strip of cloth, the coat, the tracks and the blood are the traces of the ground-reading note above (2026-10-18).
- **Area 02, the Cast stroke** — Skipped: planner chose that Rask casts ***Axefall Decree*** once, at the strongbox wagon, and that it costs him a count of three leaning on the haft, as in Chapter 003, so that the reader knows the stroke again (2026-10-18).
- **Area 01, the man in the good coat** — Skipped: planner chose that the captain glimpses him once, at the edge of the mist, as Ansel Roke did in Chapter 003, and that he is not shown again; the glimpse explains nothing, no character takes him for an informant, and since no one of the Gale caravan lives, no Gale person ever hears of him (2026-10-18).
- **Area 01, the horn** — Skipped: planner chose that the captain sounds her horn once, at the first rush, and that nothing answers, because the Ridgewatch post lies far down the road and the mist takes the note (2026-10-18).
- **Area 01, the recital of standing orders** — Skipped: planner chose that the captain's recital at the halt places her five by name and Level in a plain clause each, so that the levels reach the reader in her own thought and not as a list; the content of a Gale commander's recital is a first-use fact of the Windward Host (`gale-kingdom-military` section 6.8 holds that standing orders are spoken and kept by no copy) and the planner draws it no further (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 05 and 01, the five days** — Skipped: planner chose that the Windward Trail opened to horses a few days before the raid (no day is fixed, and the first draft's Day 10 is withdrawn because the ruling on the information ties the company's timing to the days since it opened), that the patrol finds the caravan on Day 11, on its first ride up the Seam Road since the Trail opened, and that it reaches the Aerie Palace on Day 14 because the weather stopped the Kite Line on the plateau, so that no summons went ahead and the lieutenant rode in person; the Kite Line (`aerie-plateau` section 5.2, a proposed working name) is said to end at the plateau's north edge and to stop in bad weather, which is a new first-use fact for the ledger, and it does not reach the mine road; the Trail is seasonal (`aerie-plateau` section 5.1) (2026-10-18).
- **Area 03, the districts' pictures** — Skipped: planner chose the eight one-clause pictures in the Area 03 block (Frame Row, where the storm-kite frames are bound and mended; Rack Market, where herbs dry on racks in the wind; Courier Green, where the glider pilots land and rest; Muster Field, where the Windward Host and the Wind-Sworn Militia drill; Leeside, the sheltered streets out of the easterly; Weighhouse Row, where ore and grain are weighed and sealed; Elders' Rise, where the wind-academy monasteries stand; Ropewalk, where cord and kite-line are laid and twisted) from the ledgers' own facts (herbs on racks, storm-kites, the wind-academy monasteries, the Host and the Militia); the author named the districts and accepted them, and gave no pictures (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03 and 04, the hour of the scene** — Skipped: planner chose the turn of the day for Scene S14, the late afternoon into the dusk, so that Aalia's registered dusk ward-walk (`B39-L20-PRIVATE-99`) falls in her own Area and the king goes to the open edge after the public self (`B39-L21-AFTER-99`). The author has ruled that Aeries's technique stays at dusk on the open edge (Settled); the registered late-night session of `B39-L24-SESSION-05` is the one thing the ruling departs from (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03 and 04, the two techniques** — Skipped: planner chose, from the ledger kits and by exact ledger name, for Aeries ***Vigorrondel*** (MV-SIG-GRS-010-04, Intermediate, "cleaves with qi gathered from the open air and driven through the blade", from the Windward Host text `MAN-SIG-GRS-010`, which his kit row reads as ORG:Windward Host), because it takes its qi from the open air and suits the open edge; the alternatives are ***Heroic Hiltwrap*** (MV-SIG-GRS-007-04), ***Greatsweep Grace*** (MV-SIG-GRS-007-03, a walking move) and ***Squall Greatsweep*** (MV-SIG-GRS-009-01); and for Aalia ***Nickvoyage*** (MV-SIG-SHS-009-03, Basic, Mobility:Step, "advance a pace at a time while keeping the point fixed on the named mark", from `MAN-SIG-SHS-009`, ACA:Long Coast Academy), because it is a walking move that fits her ward-walk and her doctrine that nothing is shown past need; the alternatives are ***Sainted Thumbrest*** (MV-SIG-SHS-009-01) and ***Unwavering Cutedge*** (MV-SIG-SHS-009-04). Both picks are within the level of the hero, are quiet, and are Basic or Intermediate moves of Signature-grade manuals; the Signature-grade moves of those manuals (***Famed Demolition Overswing***, ***Triumphant Haul***, ***Gallant Volition Poke***) are School Moves and would show more than a quiet practice moment, so they are not picked (2026-10-18).
- **Area 03, the letter's arrival and the letter** — Skipped: planner chose that Aegon's letter arrives at the end of Area 03 by a courier-flight pilot, who says it waited two days at the corridor post for a wind, and that the king reads it before the queen does; that the letter holds the day of the rack and no word of the ward, the road or the hurt of Phase Day 2 (the standing exclusion of Chapter 004 on those subjects is kept for the content, and only the bar on any letter is lifted); that it holds no word of his heritage; that it is in the register of a child of eight (short linked sentences of concrete things); and that it ends with one question to his father, how long yet is, which repeats in the boy's own words the question Varya asked in Chapter 004 and carries One Sky across the braid (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03 and 04, how the people see the king** — Skipped: planner chose that the king's heritage is read in the people's lowered voices and absence of kneeling, in an old petitioner's touch on the stone and a plain phrase about "the old blood", and in the birds and the dog; no form is shown; the exact phrase is the writer's (2026-10-18).
- **Area 05, Varys's letter** — Skipped: planner chose that the letter holds the raid's confirmed facts (the day and place, the number and kind of the raiders, what was taken, the dead and the one who lives, and the clamp as one plain line, Settled) and that its cause is not known, names Varys's own delay in the first person (the Court heard of the raid three weeks late, as Chapter 003 holds it, and the letter follows later still; the writing day of the letter is not pinned, so no count of days is made), and ends on the question it cannot answer; it leaves out the Court's guesses and the measures taken, so that it holds only what is true and carries no inference. The planner chose to let the clamp line say, in one clause, that the post's first dispatch was lost to it and that this is why the Court heard three weeks late, since Chapter 003 holds both as confirmed; the other reading, the clamp named with no word of the delay, is lawful. The planner reads "something true to tell" as met by a letter that is true throughout and by Varys's decision that a longer silence between friends would be worse than a telling with no answer in it (2026-10-18).
- **Area 05, the likeness of the two accounts** — Skipped: planner chose that Aeries and Aalia each see, in action, that the lieutenant's count and the letter agree in what they describe (plain coats, long hafts bound and wrapped, a company of about two dozen, nothing that names a kingdom), that nobody says "the same" and nobody names a band or a kingdom, and that the question that remains is how the raiders found each road, with the letter's one clue, the clamp, held in the king's thought and followed no further because Gale's end of the wire is absent (2026-10-18).
- **Area 05, the pilot** — Skipped: planner chose a different pilot from the pilot of Area 03, a Tier D role in both (2026-10-18).
- **Area 05, the standing-orders line** — Skipped: planner chose the wording of the one plain line, and its place near the end of the count: the Captain's company is without the orders that lived in her alone, and no one has yet spoken it new ones; the king hears it and does not answer. The ruling is that the matter is left open (Settled), so any other wording that does not say who will give the new orders, or when, is lawful (2026-10-18).
- **Area 06, the reading aloud** — Skipped: planner chose that the king reads his letter aloud once, before the hearers he names, before he seals it, so that the record is the people who heard it; this is a proposed custom drawn from the spoken-word culture of the Gale ledgers (`gale-kingdom-state` section 7.7), and the other reading is lawful: the letter's words are never shown and only his bearing and the seal are (2026-10-18).
- **Area 06, Aalia's question** — Skipped: planner chose that she asks the lieutenant one question of where (where the Captain fell, since no one lives to say where anyone lay, and since the count gave the dead but not the places), answered in full and taken away without comment; the optional beat that lets the reader see that both raids lie on roads below a ford is the planner's and may be cut (2026-10-18).
- **Area 06, Aalia's Sub-Optimal Tendency** — Skipped: planner chose to offer it as an optional beat only (`B39-L36-SUBOPT-99`, she solves by arrangement even when a plain word should be said aloud): she arranges the lieutenant's food and rest in place of a spoken thanks, and the writer and the Gate 1 decide whether it shows; no other cost of hers is planned (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03 to 06, the address settings** — Skipped: planner chose to read as public the open terrace while the Court sits or a stranger or the people stand by (Areas 03 and 05, and Area 06 on the terrace while the lieutenant, the pilot, the steward and the Court's people are present), where Gale people say "Your Majesty", and as private the ward-line and the palace's inner places and any moment alone with the king or the queen, where they say "Sire" and "Madam" (Area 04, and Area 06 once each goes to their own rooms). Only the author's rule is used: "Sire" and "Madam" in private and "Your Majesty" in public; no form is extended to the Azure Sky couple (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 to 06, openings and closings** — Skipped: planner chose, from the fixed lists, the opening types cut to another thread, continues directly, cut to another thread, continues directly, time jump and continues directly, and the closing types cliffhanger, question left open, turn or reveal, quiet landing, turn or reveal and question left open; no two consecutive Areas open or close the same way (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, the collective code of Stirrup Ford and the Seam Road** — Skipped: planner chose none for the ford and the road: they are new places with no location file yet, and the named people who act there already carry their own codes (the escort's THE RECITAL, registered 2026-10-18, and the Redhaft Vanguard's registered code). The author's ruling of none covers Highgale and the Ridgewatch patrol only (Settled); for the ford and the road, none is the planner's reading, and the author may ask for a code for either place or rule none. The final sweep re-lists this note for the author's last chance (2026-10-18).

**Names**
- **Header, title "The House With No Walls"** — Name: kept by the author 2026-10-18; a Chapter name is a label until its first Gate 1 delivery (Part 28 Rule 18.4.4); the title is the Novel-Part entry's working label, unchanged.
- **Section 5, Area titles (First Light at Stirrup Ford; The Count and the Sign; The Wind Over Highgale; The Ward-Walk at Dusk; What the Pilot Carried; Two Seals)** — Name: proposed by the planner as working labels until the first Gate 1 delivery; the author may rename any of them at the confirm (2026-10-18).
- **Area 01 onward, Whistledeep** — Name: the author's pick, 2026-10-18; MAJOR if the mine recurs at Section scale (Part 28 Rule 20.4). A whole-word, case-insensitive scan of 2026-10-18 over the ledgers, skills, specs and written Areas outside the archive, agent memory and `.git` finds no hit. A proposal until its location file is written at the Gate 2.
- **Areas 01, 02 and 05, the Seam Road, Stirrup Ford, Shalegate Pass and the Mule Stair** — Name: proposed by the planner from the author's cleared set; the same scan finds no hit for any of the four; each is a MINOR place unless it recurs at Section scale (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 to 06, Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott and Egil Hurn** — Name: the author's, 2026-10-18; MINOR (Tier D by the author's ruling). Each first name and surname was scanned as a whole word over the same scope, with no hit and no echo that needs a change.
- **Areas 05 and 06, Dagmar Sund** — Name: the author's, 2026-10-18; MINOR (Tier D by the author's ruling). The scan finds no hit for "Dagmar" or "Sund" as a whole word.
- **Areas 01 and 02, Hadric Stoll** — Name: an existing name of Chapter 003 (Tier C side villain; `ledger-character-villain/_index.md`); no new name is made for him, and no other raider is named in this spec (2026-10-18).
- **Area 03 onward, Highgale and its eight districts** — Name: Highgale is the author's own name for the Gale capital, made in the ledger on 2026-10-18 (MAJOR, consulted; `highgale`), and Frame Row, Rack Market, Courier Green, Muster Field, Leeside, Weighhouse Row, Elders' Rise and Ropewalk are the author's, accepted 2026-10-18; each is MAJOR if it recurs at Section scale. The scan of 2026-10-18 finds no hit for any district name. Their Establishment status is a proposal until the Gate 2 writes the first Area that shows them.
- **Areas 03, 05 and 06, the Stone Ring** — Name: the author's own name, 2026-10-18, for the ring of stones at the Aerie Palace precinct where the Court's stones stand and the Stone Tribunal sits. It replaces the ledger's proposed working name "Witness Ring" (`aerie-palace` section 5.2, where the ring's own name is OPEN-AUTHOR). The scan of 2026-10-18 finds no place, person, faction or item of that name; the echoes are the Manual Ledger's retired old name "Sill Stone Ring Setting" in the rename map (now ***Intentarm***, MV-ELI-TRB-008-04) and cultivation move names that contain the word Ring (for example ***Endless Ring***, ***Repel Ring***); none is a place and none is a Gale weapon move. The Gate 1 checker repeats the scan and the move-register check (Check #17(b)); no Area uses "Ring" as the name of a move.
- **Areas 01 and 05, Ridgewatch, the Windward Host, the Aerie Artificers' College, the Kite Line and the Windward Trail** — Name: "Ridgewatch" is the author's descriptor for the detachment, scanned clear; the Windward Host and the Aerie Artificers' College are existing ledger names (`gale-kingdom-military`; the Manual Ledger institution rows); the Kite Line and the Windward Trail are existing ledger names, the Kite Line a proposed working name in `aerie-plateau` section 5.2 (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 to 04, the Named Moves** — Name: every move this Chapter plans is taken from the Manual Ledger by its exact registered name and ID, including ***Vigorrondel*** and ***Nickvoyage***; none is proposed or coined, so the Move-Naming Law (Part 42 Rule 42.12.2.3) is not engaged. Rask's ***Subjugating Hookback*** and ***Axefall Decree*** are registered from Chapter 003 (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 to 06, the Tier D persons** — Name: the seven Gale people are named by the author and every other Tier D person is planned by role; the drafting agent picks the names of the others at the writing, scans them and enters the Tier D rows at the one Gate 2, with the codes picked for all of them, the seven included (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, Power Crystal** — Name: an existing substance of the energy law and no new name.

**Open (items no answer in this build can close: ledger-side registrations and writes, blocking registrations, and flags for the author; the confirm step re-lists each)**
- **Areas 01 and 02, the stage of Hadric Stoll's Level 6 (`OPEN-AUTHOR`)** — Open: Hadric Stoll is a Warrior of the sixth Level, and his stage (Low, Mid, High or Peak) is the author's to set (`ledger-character-villain/_index.md`, the Tier C row and its open fields; the page of Chapter 003 gives the Level and no stage). The author's answer of 2026-10-18 on it named Rask as the Vanguard's leader on the field and gave no stage, so the stage stays unset and the plan does not set it by implication: the Engagement Ledgers read his Level as a range, Level 6 Low to Level 6 Peak (positions 20 to 23 by Part 44 Rule 44.2.3.1, against the captain's 26), which puts the captain above him by 3 to 6 steps whichever stage he holds (Shut, the company below), and the page gives him "the sixth" with no stage. The first draft's Level 6 High for the squad leader is withdrawn. His address forms are also `OPEN-AUTHOR` (no Brackenhold address register exists), and the plan has him use and receive none. The author sets the stage, or the Chapter leaves it open and the writing gives none.
- **Areas 01 and 02, the map: Brackenhold's frontier with Gale** — Open: the author's ruling places the raid at the foot of the Windward Range on Gale's northern frontier with Brackenhold. The ledgers' map rulings of 2026-10-18 place Brackenhold north-west of Azure Sky across the lower ford, bordering Azure Sky and the Ember Reach polities, and hold no border between Gale and Brackenhold (`brackenhold` Section 1, Territorial Extent; `windward-range` section 5.1: one range running west to east along the north of both kingdoms, the Aerie Plateau tucked under its eastern stretch and Gale's climb through the Stormbreak Foothills). The Chapter's placement is therefore in no ledger yet and widens Brackenhold's extent. Owed at the Gate 2 and to the author: a map ruling that adds Gale's northern frontier to Brackenhold's extent; the location files for Whistledeep, the Seam Road, Stirrup Ford, Shalegate Pass and the Mule Stair, and the lines of `windward-range`, `aerie-plateau` and `gale-kingdom` for the foot and the frontier (whether the Windward Trail runs through Shalegate Pass is `OPEN-AUTHOR`, `aerie-plateau` section 5.1); and the fuller Brackenhold ledger file the author asked for. Until then the ledger stands for established facts and this placement is a plan fact. The author's Brackenhold matter is not touched by any of it.
- **Whole spec, the address ruling: ledger text to change (for the ledger step at the Gate 2)** — Open: the author's ruling of 2026-10-18 supersedes ledger text that the planner's search of 2026-10-18 finds in these files, by name only, for the continuity-checker's impact report to confirm and list by line: `aeries` Section 7 (the Gale Relational Address System block: "What He Is Called", "How He Completes an Acknowledgment", the error list, whose first row makes "Your Majesty" from an official a CRITICAL error, and the rows that use "Wind-Crown" and "Storm-Counsel"); `aalia` Section 7 (the matching block); `aegon` Section 7 (the row that bars "Your Majesty" from officials); `gale-kingdom-state` section G16 (the acknowledgment system, the office-noun "Wind-Crown", the "Storm-Counsel" office-noun and the CRITICAL rows on "Your Majesty"); `gale-kingdom-military` section 6.11 (the decision that no "sir" or "ma'am" form exists in the Gale military; the bare rank noun stays and is what the ruling keeps for ranks); `aerie-palace` Section 3 (Controlling Faction: "the Wind-Crown (Aeries) and Storm-Counsel (Aalia)"); and the side-character index rows for Gale persons. The ruling keeps the bare rank noun, birth names, "Sire", "Madam" and "Your Majesty", and sets aside the acknowledgment, wind-names and the two office-nouns. This spec makes none of those edits and establishes no ledger value (Rule 1.3.2); where the ledger and the ruling differ on an established fact, the ledger stands until the step is done, and the plan follows the ruling meanwhile.
- **Areas 03 and 04, Aeries's costly behavior** — Open: `B39-L36-COST` Costly Behavior 2 (he spends himself on reachability) is "paid on the page at: not yet" in his file; the hearing that runs past its hour in Area 03 is planned as its first payment, and the author may defer the payment to a later Chapter. The Behavioral Plan and the Planted here line of Area 03 say the same: planned, and the author may defer.

The author decides chapter by chapter; the answer of 2026-10-02 is not replaced (2026-10-18).

This spec makes none of those edits and establishes no ledger value.

**Variance (departures from the Novel-Part Spec entry, each with the entry's original beside it; the author's answer on each is asked at the confirm step, and a variance note goes on every spec above this Chapter, the Section Spec first and then the Novel-Part Spec; Part 38 Rules 9.4.3 and 9.4.5)**
- **Header, Key events and Threads Carried Out (Chapter 005 entry, Key events; Threads out, The Friendship)** — Variance: the entry shows Aegon's home "by his absence, in the answers" and keeps him off the page. This spec adds a letter from Aegon, written on the evening of Phase Day 8 and read on Phase Day 12 in Areas 03 and 04, so that his home is shown by his absence and by his own words. It lifts the exclusion of Chapters 003 and 004 on any letter from Aegon to Gale, for this Chapter only. The author asked for the letter. The author's answer on whether to amend the entry: leave the entry as the plan baseline (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step); the variance notes are on Section Spec 1.1 and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (2026-10-18).
- **Header, On the page (Chapter 005 entry, On the page)** — Variance: the entry names "a patrol's messenger with his report and the carrier of Varys's letter" as unnamed Tier D newcomers. This spec makes the patrol's reporter a named person, Dagmar Sund, a woman and a Lieutenant of the Windward Host's Ridgewatch detachment (the author's ruling of 2026-10-18), Tier D by the author's ruling, an officer who gives the report herself in place of a messenger; the carrier of Varys's letter stays a Tier D pilot. The author's answer on whether to amend the entry: leave the entry as the plan baseline (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step); the variance notes are on Section Spec 1.1 and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (2026-10-18).
- **Header and Areas 01, 02, 05 and 06, the tier of the Gale people (Chapter 005 entry, Key events and On the page)** — Variance: the entry calls the escort's captain and five warriors "all named people of Tier C" and says the people of Tier C who act are "named at the Chapter Spec and registered before drafting". The author ruled on 2026-10-18 that all seven Gale people (the six of the escort and Dagmar Sund) are Tier D, so no registration is owed and each takes a registry row with a picked code at the one Gate 2. The author's answer on whether to amend the entry: leave the entry as the plan baseline (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step); the variance notes are on Section Spec 1.1 and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (2026-10-18).
- **Header, On the page, Hadric Stoll (Chapter 005 entry, On the page)** — Variance (information): the entry names Wulfric Rask as the company's leader and no other raider; this spec adds Hadric Stoll, the Tier C side villain of Chapter 003, as one of the two squad leaders, named in Area 02 only and seen unnamed in Area 01. The entry stays the plan baseline (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01, 02 and 05, what the Gale court knows of the raiders (Section Spec 1.1, blocks 7 and 8; Chapter 005 entry, Key events)** — Variance (information): Section Spec 1.1 (blocks 7 and 8) says the Gale court learns of the raid "as plain-clothes raiders and no more", and the Novel-Part Spec entry says the people of Gale "know the raiders only as men in plain clothes, unknown". By the author's ruling of 2026-10-18 this spec keeps the ground-reading: the patrol reads plain coats, long hafts, a company of about two dozen, a mule-track and the raiders' own dead carried out, and still no source, no band and no kingdom. The author's answer on whether to amend the Section Spec and the entry or to keep their words as the plan baseline: keep their words as the plan baseline (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step); the variance notes are on Section Spec 1.1 and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (2026-10-18).
- **Header and Areas 05 and 06, the clamp in Varys's letter and the clue Gale holds (Chapter 005 entry, Reveals and payoffs owed; Key events; Threads out, The Lone Banner)** — Variance (information): the entry gives Gale "two reports and the same question" and says Varys's letter carries "no new event"; it names no clue. This spec adds, by the author's ruling of 2026-10-18, the clamp of Chapter 003 as one plain confirmed fact in the letter, so Gale holds one clue to the leak that it cannot test. The clamp is not a new event (Chapter 003 holds it as confirmed), and no source, band or kingdom is named, so the entry's "learns no source for either" stands. Chapter Spec 1.1.1.003 plans the clamp's return in a later Novel-Part with the leak; this Chapter carries it once to Gale and plans no return of it here. The author's answer on whether to amend the entry: leave the entry as the plan baseline (answered 2026-10-18, at the confirm step); the variance notes are on Section Spec 1.1 and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (2026-10-18).
- **Header, Threads Carried In (Chapter 005 entry, Threads in)** — Variance (information): the entry's Threads in do not list The Friendship or row 13; this spec lists both, as a reading of the entry's own Threads out (The Friendship carried; the hidden edge held back), as Chapter Spec 002 did for One Sky. The entry stays the plan baseline (2026-10-18).
- **Sections 1 and 5, the Court, the Stone Ring and the open terrace (Chapter 005 entry, Key events)** — Variance (information): the entry speaks of "the court's people" and "the Gale court" and names no place of the Court. This spec holds the Court on the open terrace of the Aerie Palace as a descriptor, with no named hall, and names the Court's stones the Stone Ring (the author's name). The entry's purpose, calendar window, reveals owed and closing beat are as they were (2026-10-18).
- **Chapter Specs 1.1.1.002, 1.1.1.003 and 1.1.1.004 (COMPLETED), words-only notes (the first two carried from Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1's cascade report of 2026-10-18; the Chapter 003 note added by this spec)** — Variance (information): Chapter Spec 1.1.1.003 plans the clamp and the lost first dispatch to return in Novel-Part 3 with the leak, and this Chapter carries the clamp once to Gale in Varys's letter; Chapter Spec 1.1.1.004's Inherited note "Whole spec, the cut plan" reads that Chapter 005 opens in Gale among courier-flights, and this Chapter opens on a road at Gale's northern frontier; Chapter Spec 1.1.1.002's note on the opposing force reads "once, back-dated... and none in the village line", and the force is now on the page twice. The orchestrating command may append a words-only Variance note to each; both specs stay COMPLETED, and written Areas are not touched (Part 38 Rule 9.4.2) (2026-10-18).

**Audit**
- **Whole spec, the chain** — Audit: Section Spec 1.1 (CONFIRMED, v1.4) and Novel-Part Spec 1.1.1 (CONFIRMED, v1.6) are in force (Rule 9.1.1). Both were amended on 2026-10-18 on the author's instruction to make the edits directly, so neither had a separate light audit or a final sweep before it was written; those are owed to the next Continuity Checker pass, and anything they return reopens the lines they name (2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, standing exclusions** — Audit: Screened at draft against section 3 and the Chapter 003 and 004 exclusions: no Law P30-L8 entity (the ground north of the Range is not named, shown or hinted at, and the raiders' track goes up the draw, is lost on the shale and is never followed), no sentient monster or allusion to one, no Sapphire Line wording (no hum, frequency, vibration, resonance or bedrock wording for any wire, relay, ward or mine), no hint of the matter the author holds back about Brackenhold, no form or part-form of either sovereign, no night flight, no child on the page and no source or kingdom named by a Gale person (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01 and 02, Part 44 checklist** — Audit: (1) Anchors: Rask, Warrior Level 9 Peak (35), over the captain, Warrior Level 7 High (26); (2) Gaps: Rask over the captain 9 (Shut, the captain below), the captain over the unnamed squad leader 2 (Open) and over Hadric Stoll 3 to 6, whichever stage he holds (Shut, the company below), the lines over the escort's warriors 0 to 8 (Open to Shut, the escort below or level); (3) Presence: no Ruler on the field, so no aura collapses the hands; each fighter's qi lasts a short skirmish; (4) Offset: numbers (a) and ambush with preparation (b) carry every pairing the company wins, with a small part of exhaustion (d) for the captain's spent breath; the kind of information source, the time and the place of the ambush are named in the Engagement Ledgers (Rule 44.6.2.2), and which source it was is not chosen and is left to Novel-Part 3 by the author's ruling; (5) Price: the escort wholly, all five hands, five line fighters of the company, and Rask's one Cast stroke; (6) Outcome: matches the levels, the higher side wins every Shut pairing, TT-1 and TT-4, VC-1; (7) Strain: none under Part 44, and no Part 39 item is open for the fight, since Rask's kill state is registered (`B39-L25-KILL-02`) (2026-10-18).
- **Header and Areas, the calendar** — Audit: Day 1 is Orvil (the calendar ledger), so Day 9 is Nuvil, Day 12 is Alovil and Day 14 is Mavil (Part 41 Area 3, by the formula, checked against the written Chapters: Day 2 Nuvil, Day 8 Orvil). Every date falls on or after the day of the Area before it, none steps back, and each nests inside the Chapter window (Day 9 to Day 14), the Novel-Part window (Day 1 to Day 92) and the Section window. The Phase Day of each date equals its day of Ilovany, since Ilovany is the first month (Part 41 Area 4) (2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, Part 30 coverage and ceilings** — Audit: the early part of the Rival-Kingdom Raids and Skirmishes function (the early raid Part 30 admits in the first fortnight), World Introduction, Ensemble Introduction (Aeries and Aalia), First Cultivation Scenes (one technique each), The Alliance Established (traces) and the technology thread (Gale's end by absence) are all carried; no kingdom declares war; no strike falls on Azorion; nobody is shown above a Section 1 close band (Aeries is Ruler Level 2 Mid and Aalia Ruler Level 1 Mid at the start, and Part 30's close bands are Ruler Level 3 Mid and Ruler Level 2 High, so no breakthrough is planned or needed) (2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, Behavioral Plans** — Audit: every Area block carries a Behavioral Plan (Part 38 Rule 6.1.2). The acting appearances of Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott, Egil Hurn and Dagmar Sund are Tier D by the author's ruling of 2026-10-18, which needs a registry row and a code picked at the writing and no Layer registration (Part 39 Rule 39.4.3.1a), so none of them is a blocking item. Sigrun Korth is a Tier D viewpoint holder by the same ruling, and her Layers L02 and L07 are not registered, which the Gate 1 reads as information. Hadric Stoll is Tier C and registered (the next note), and Wulfric Rask, as a viewpoint holder, is registered (his own note below). No blocking item stands: the two collective codes, the Aerie Palace's (THE OPEN EDGE) and the mine escort's (THE RECITAL), are confirmed by the author with their wording (Settled), and the ledger lane writes their blocks before drafting; the ford and the road carry the planner's reading of none (Skipped). The deaths and consequences of the Tier D persons were put to the author under Part 19 Rule 19.1.2.4 and ruled as planned (Settled). No Tier D person is planned to make a consequential choice; the squad leaders pin the captain and do not decide her, and Rask ends her. Anticipated drift is a plan and not an establishment (2026-10-18).
- **Area 02, Hadric Stoll's registration and the Threshold C-2 note** — Audit: Hadric Stoll is registered at Tier C (core six, L02, L07 and the Signature `B39-L03-CHANNEL-03`), so a planned acting appearance is lawful (`ledger-character-villain/_index.md`, the Tier C row and its behavioral line, Part 39 Rule 39.6.1.2); the Threshold C-2 question was answered for him on 2026-10-08 and fires once per character, so it is not asked again; his Chapter 003 Check #21 ruling was KEEP PROFILE. The plan uses only registered lines of his file for him, his Signature as the exactness that grows under pressure, his order given again in ordered steps and his registered move ***Staticsnatch*** (***Brawnhaft***, his other registered move, is the first rank's pull of the sacking in Area 01), and plans no new trait, no L10 or L08 value and no address form; the stage of his Level 6 and his address forms are `OPEN-AUTHOR` (section 7, Open). He acts in Area 02 as a named person inside Rask's viewpoint; the Gate 1 reads his Area 02 appearance against the Tier C row and the Check #21 enquiry of his Chapter 003 ruling (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 05 and 06, the shape of the lieutenant's report** — Audit: the author's "ordinary count" is met in content and the dialogue rule is met in shape: the report carries the numbers, the places, the days and the traces of the ground inside full, connected sentences and is never a tally, a list or a clipped run; the Maker gives it the length the matter takes (Part 29 Rules 29.2.1.7 and 29.2.1.7a). The traces are many by plan, and the Maker weaves them into the count and may leave out any that the length does not bear, keeping at least the plain coats, the long hafts and the number, which carry the likeness with the letter (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 01, 02 and 05, the ground-reading against the ledgers** — Audit: the report gives only what the ground gives (about two dozen where the ledgers hold twenty-four; the escort of six and the five hands dead; the company's dead carried out; the ore and the manifest taken), so no line of it contradicts the Engagement Ledgers (Part 44 Rule 44.9.2.1); two of the traces are shown on the page before they are read in Area 05 (Area 01 anchors 4 and 5), and the other three come from events of Area 02 (anchors 1 and 7a) and are never shown as traces; every trace is a thing the Vanguard's code leaves uncovered (it covers the mark, the name and the cry and not the ground) or a small miss of a clearing that follows Rask's Operational Belief, "nothing is left on the road" (`wulfric-rask` Section 4), and none names a kingdom (2026-10-18).
- **Area 02, Wulfric Rask's registration** — Audit: met by the ledger step of 2026-10-18 (relayed after the first draft): the villain file `ledger-character-villain/wulfric-rask.md` (v2.0), Layers L02 and L07 complete for his viewpoint, the Tier B interview held, the twelve Signatures, the two contradiction pairs, the costly behavior and the kill state registered; so the registration notes of the first draft (the villain filing, the viewpoint Layers, the interview, the Voice Register scope) are withdrawn and no registration is owed for him. His Voice Register is the author's ruling (assertive and command-oriented, flowing and not abrupt, in speech and in thought), and the Tone line of Area 02 follows it and names no other trait of his voice. The lines of his file that the author left open are not registered, and no Behavioral Plan row relies on any of them. What stays open for him is not blocking: any line of his file still flagged for the author when Area 02 is drafted (Open, the Check #21 note) and the Manual Ledger rows owed a Tier B person (Open, the eligibility note) (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 04 and 06, Aalia's viewpoint** — Audit: her interior is imagery and placement and never sentences, and the faculty is shown as conduct and never narrated (the rendering constraint of section 3); her letters are the translation made afterward in words for another person, which her ledger allows (`B39-L07-MODE-99`). Drafting her viewpoint without a spoken or thought reasoning is the main craft risk of the Chapter (2026-10-18).
- **Areas 03 and 05, Aeries's register** — Audit: the roll before the break (at least one full rolling sentence before any humor fragment), the pause only before a promise, the alarm register once, the sentence restarted from its beginning when interrupted and the exact quotation as a marked act are the main craft risks in his speech (2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, the Gale spoken-word culture** — Audit: Aeries "will not act on a written account until a voice stands behind it" is what makes the pilot's word the voice behind Varys's paper, and the reading aloud of the reply is the proposed way a Gale hand answers in writing; neither is a ledger fact beyond `B39-L06-INFO-99` and `gale-kingdom-state` section 7.7 (2026-10-18).
- **Header, Standing question of rank and level words** — Audit: "Captain" and "Lieutenant" are rungs of the Windward Host's ladder (`gale-kingdom-military` section 6.1) used as bare rank nouns; the ledger assigns no realm to any post, so the captain's Level 7 High and the lieutenant's Senior Level 9 High are their own and not their posts'; "guard-captain" of Chapter 003 is a trade post of another kingdom and is not used (2026-10-18).
- **Whole spec, the hand-off to Chapter 006** — Audit: Chapter 006 opens on a plain change of line to the border village at dusk (Novel-Part Spec cut plan, row 005); the story clock stands on Phase Day 14 at the close, and Chapter 006 is planned in Melorany (Phase Day 29 to Phase Day 59), so a gap follows (2026-10-18).

The Spec Version stays v1.0 and the header is not touched; N8 was not part of the direction and is not applied here.
