- **Header, Last Sync, Spec Status and the New named things line** — Audit: The Last Sync line names this sync; the Spec Status reads COMPLETED and the header gains a Completed date; the line "New named things met by the reader this Chapter" is completed from the written Chapter; the Built-against stamp gains a sentence that leaves the ledger entries and the calendar rows to the ledger lane. The Light Continuity Audit line is left as the history of the build (2026-10-19).
- **Header, Chapter size target (words) and the word band** — Audit: Build mode whole stands. The word band of Rule 2.3.10 was read in place of the size range (Rule 3.6.1 item 7). Counting method: a word is a whitespace token that holds a word character; date lines, hidden Area markers and break marks are not counted. Figures (the Maker's, given for the final text by the orchestrating command, the script `measure` being the figure of record; this lane ran no script and holds no shell): Area 01 2,124 of 3,000 (70.8 percent), Area 02 1,866 (62.2), Area 03 2,083 (69.4), Area 04 1,707 (56.9), Area 05 2,052 (68.4), Area 06 1,702 (56.7); Chapter 11,534 of T = 18,000 (64.1 percent, inside 9,900 to 12,960). By the Maker's pinned counts the file holds 11,584 tokens from the first marker on, of which 50 are not narration (the six markers at four tokens each, 24; the five `---` breaks, 5; the three date lines at six tokens each, 18; and the one `* * *`, 3), which leaves 11,534. Every Area is inside the 55 to 72 percent band its Budget stretch names (none below 1,650 words and none above 2,160 words), and the Chapter lands 1,426 words under the ceiling; no band exception was needed. Read against the Area band of canon v10.0 (1,280 to 2,400 words per Area), which a whole-built Chapter does not apply, and as information only: all six Areas fall inside it. Nothing is asked of the author and no block follows (2026-10-19).
- **Section 2, Tier Scope Bracket (Areas in this Chapter, 5 to 7)** — Audit: Six Area blocks were written, inside the range; no readjustment is owed (2026-10-19).
- **Section 5.0, Scene Map (as built)** — Audit: Compared with the written text. S13 holds Areas 01 and 02 as one scene on Day 9 (Fast), with the Day 9 date line at the top of the Chapter and one visible break inside it before Rask's viewpoint; S14 holds Areas 03 and 04 on Day 12 (Slow), with a break and the Day 12 date line before Area 03 and a break before Aalia's viewpoint; S15 holds Areas 05 and 06 on Day 14 (Medium), with a break and the Day 14 date line before Area 05 and a break before Aalia's viewpoint. The file shows five `---` breaks after the title block and three date lines, as the Scene Map plans (Rule 2.4.12), and the one planned `* * *` stands once, inside Area 06, after the king takes his long pause. The joins as built: 01 to 02 holds (the captain on the gravel bar, the horn sounded once, a weight far above her coming out of the trees; Area 02 opens on Rask at the trees' edge in the last moments before he goes down to the bar, with two of the centre rank already at her feet, D1); 03 to 04 holds (the first of the Stone Vigil's lamps caught and the air breaking around someone walking the ward-line, late and calm; Area 04 opens with the Vigil's lamp lit and the queen late at the first station); 05 to 06 holds (the lieutenant at the end of her count, Varys's letter lying open on the Court's table, the king's question asked and unanswered, the queen not gone back to her place, a step nearer the king). The Scene Map needs no edit. Scene word figures against the caps (4,320 each): S13 3,990, S14 3,790, S15 3,754; information only (2026-10-19).
- **Section 5.0a, Cultivation Visibility (as built)** — Audit: Compared with the written text. Every named person who appears has a stated level or a worded range and a route, woven into scene and speech and never as a list. Sigrun Korth ***Warrior Level 7 High*** (a string in her own thought) and her five by name and Level in her recital (Gant fourth Level, high; Wold fourth, middle; Ulm fourth, low; Mott third, the top of it; Hurn third, middle); the carters and the clerk read as absence by her sense; the company's ranks as worded ranges in her reading and in Rask's count (the fifth Level, low and middle; the fourth, middle and high; the third, high, and the fourth, low), the two leaders as one a little under her and one further under, then as the sixth Level (Hadric Stoll, no stage) and the seventh, low; Rask ***Warrior Level 9 Peak*** (a string in his own viewpoint), far above the captain, her knees aching before his stroke; Aeries ***Ruler Level 2 Mid*** and Aalia ***Ruler Level 1 Mid*** (a string in each viewpoint, each holding qi in among others); Ranulf Skeld, the two petitioners of Area 03 and Knut Dahl shown unawakened (no qi in them to read; "a stout unawakened man"), and Ragna Holm "an unawakened woman"; Ivar Lund and Sverre Vik each a Senior of the lower Levels (a clause); Dagmar Sund ***Senior Level 9 High*** (a string, her weight drawn down before a Ruler); Gudrun Nyland a Senior of the middle Levels and Leif Bakke a Senior of the upper Levels (a clause each; the plan had the Stone Vigil's garrison carry none, D11). The man in the good coat carries no level (a glimpse). No sealed or hidden level is on the page, and by this lane's reading no sentence states a step or a gap in minor steps (Check #23 element 23-7 is Gate 1's) (2026-10-19).

- **Area 02, anchor 1 and the Opening type line, the opening (D1)** — Variance: Planned: the Area runs in motion from the last moment of Area 01, a few breaths back so that the reader sees the sign that started the raid (Rask on the near bank as the trees stood, the company in a line with the hafts stood butt-down in the soft ground, his count of the file in his own thought, his right hand rising to send the first rank and a second lift to send the centre), and the Opening type line says it "runs a few breaths back to the sign and forward to the weight walking out". Written: the Area opens live on Rask at the trees' edge, reading the gravel bar in the last moments before he goes down to it (the captain a steady weight a little above anything else there, two of the centre rank already at her feet and a third going down), and gives the count-in and the sign straight after as recollection in two short paragraphs: the count made before the light, with the ranks and Levels, and then the sign (the hand to the shoulder and down once, a second lift for the centre), the eleven heartbeats of silence after the horn and the spoken order. The join with Area 01 holds (Scene Map, S13). The written value replaces the planned value in anchor 1 and in the Opening type line; the planned value is kept in this note. Recorded from the Maker's hand-off (its join report) and carried by the author's `[CONFIRM WRITE]` (2026-10-19).
- **Area 03, anchor 1, the sweep (D2)** — Variance: Planned: "the descent is one paragraph or two and not a tour". Written: three paragraphs (the wind, the plateau and the masts of the *Kite Line*; the eight districts, each met by one thing read in it and one clause of picture; the palace with no walls, the ward-line, the Stone Ring and the man), and none is a tour, since each district takes a clause. The written value replaces the planned value in anchor 1; the planned value is kept in this note. Recorded from the Maker's hand-off and carried by the author's `[CONFIRM WRITE]` (2026-10-19).
- **Area 03, Behavioral Plan (Contradiction or cost rendered) and Planted here, the hearing past its hour (D3)** — Variance: Planned: Costly Behavior 2, "he spends himself on reachability" (`B39-L36-COST`): the hearing that runs past its hour and takes the evening's margin, planned as the first payment on the page, with the note that the author may defer it. Written: the hearing is rendered (he crosses to the last two petitioners, hears each out in full and remembers a name from years back), but not as a cost; the page shows no margin spent and no evening lost, only the light going. The first payment of `B39-L36-COST` is therefore not on the page. The author deferred the payment (relayed by the orchestrating command, 2026-10-19; the Open note of the build named a later Chapter as its place). The Open note "Areas 03 and 04, Aeries's costly behavior" is answered by this deferral (the Deferred note below), and the Planted here line of Area 03 says the cost is not rendered (2026-10-19).
- **Area 03, anchor 1, the king's look (D4)** — Variance (information): Planned: the Area block lists no physical detail of Aeries. Written: the wind lifts his silver-black hair at the open edge, the first physical detail of him on the page. It equals the ledger's own line (`aeries` Section 1, Hair: silver-black), so nothing conflicts; the Gate 2 ledger step marks that line as shown in prose. The grey of his eyes going deeper (Area 03) matches the ledger's Eyes line (deep overcast grey) (2026-10-19).
- **Area 04, anchor 5 and the Signature row `B39-L01-PROX-99`, the queen's distance (D5)** — Variance: Planned: "she stands a step nearer than she would stand to a stranger (her joy shortens distances)", and the Signature row's beat "a step nearer than to a stranger". Written: "She came to rest beside him, close", and, in the talk, "She stood nearer, until her shoulder was against his arm". Author-ruled (relayed by the orchestrating command, 2026-10-19). The Signature row is rendered from the text as written, and no drift entry is owed. The written value replaces the planned value in anchor 5 and in the row; the planned value is kept in this note (2026-10-19).
- **Area 04, anchor 4, the second station (D6)** — Variance (information): Planned: the Area block lists no memory of the boy at a station; anchor 4 is the boot by the door. Written: the second station adds a memory in her viewpoint, a place-first memory and not a scene: on the smooth place on the shoulder of the mark, "a boy of five who would not go on until she told him what the stone was for"; she had said it was the edge, and "the edge of here" when he asked the edge of what. It adds no event. The Gate 2 ledger step records it in `aegon` and `aalia` as a past moment (Aegon at five, on the ward-line at the second station) and nothing more (2026-10-19).
- **Areas 04 and 06, Aalia's "gate" lines (D7)** — Variance (information): Planned: in Area 04 she answers "with a place where a time was asked, lightly", and Valia's four lines are to be answered "tomorrow, or the day after"; in Area 06 her letter to Valia gives the answer first. Written: "We will go to the gate." (Area 04, to the king) and "The third I will answer at the gate, because I cannot say it in a letter." (Area 06, in her letter to Valia) are her private intent. No journey, date, plan or promise follows: the king takes the place as the first answer anyone has put in his hand that he could walk to, nothing is sworn, and his letter to Varys binds nothing. The Gate 2 ledger step records stated intent only: no scheduled visit to Azorion, no travel event and no commitment row (2026-10-19).
- **Area 05, anchor 3, the eyes (D8)** — Variance: Planned: when the king has read Varys's letter, "he goes rigid and silent for one breath, then takes a full one, and the grey of his eyes goes deeper for a second; the Court looks away". Written: he does not move for one breath, takes a full one, and his hands settle, and the Court looks away (Ottar Haug studies the planks, the steward turns his face to the Ring, Knut Dahl goes deep into his slate); the eyes beat of Area 03 is not repeated, so the dimension `B39-L03-LEAK-99` listed for Area 05 in the Behavioral Plan is not shown in this Area. A staging difference. The written value replaces the planned value in anchor 3; the planned value is kept in this note (2026-10-19).
- **Area 06, anchor 2, the queen's thought on the Captain's company's orders (D9)** — Variance: Planned: the queen takes the answer away and "says nothing of what she makes of it (the conclusion, if she has one, is absent and not withheld; no reasoning is given or sought)", and the Settled note on the Captain's company's standing orders says the Chapter does not say who gives the company new orders or when. Written: she lays the gravel bar beside the road below the lower ford ("Two fords."), says nothing of it, and thinks of the Captain's company standing "with a hole in the middle where its orders had been", and she "left the hole unfilled, because the filling was not hers to do until the king had spoken it, standing, before named witnesses". The author kept the clause on purpose (relayed by the orchestrating command, 2026-10-19). It is the queen's understanding of how it will be done once someone does it: the king will speak the orders, standing, before named witnesses. No new order is given in the Chapter, no rule of the Host is stated, the king makes no ruling or promise on it, and what the new orders are and when they are given stays open; the king hears the plain line in Area 05 and does not answer it. The Settled note on the standing orders is overtaken in part, for who and how, and stays above as history. For the Gate 2 ledger step: the first-use line in `gale-kingdom-military` for the loss of a Captain's company's standing orders, and any line in `aalia`, `aeries` or `aerie-palace`, must say that this is the queen's understanding only, in her viewpoint in Area 06; it must not record an order given, a rule of the Host, a ruling or promise of the king, or an answer to the open matter. The written value is added to anchor 2; the planned value is kept in this note (2026-10-19).
- **Area 06, anchor 7 and the Continuation, the last image (D10)** — Variance: Planned: the two seals are set in two ways in two rooms, "the question of how the raiders found out lies on both desks, unanswered", and the Continuation reads that "both letters are sealed and in the pilot's hands, the king and the queen are in two rooms of the house". Written: the two sealings stay apart (the king's after his long pause, which the queen hears end as a latch, a chair, a step, a door and a stool scraping on the terrace; the queen's in her own rooms two hours on, pressed when the air moves), and then the queen, at her window, sees the pilot take both letters on the terrace, hers from the boy's hand and the king's from the king's own, look from the one seal to the other and put them inside his coat together, with the question under the wax of each and no answer in either; the wind has turned. The king is on the terrace at the end and the queen in her rooms. The Chapter's closing beat (two answers written in two rooms, the question standing) is met, and the Chapter goes one image beyond it. The written value replaces the planned value in anchor 7 and in the Continuation; the planned value is kept in this note (2026-10-19).
- **Areas 03 to 06, the Tier D persons named by the writing, and two levels (D11)** — Variance (information): Planned: a Chapter Spec names no Tier D person beyond the author's seven Gale people (the steward, the petitioners, the pilots, the keeper of the third station, the Court's people and the garrison are roles), and block 5.0a has the Stone Vigil's garrison carry no level. Written: the drafting pass named nine more, MINOR persons of Tier D, by the Part 28 Area 20.3 method with no author consultation owed: Ranulf Skeld (the Court's steward), Ottar Haug (a farmer with a fallen terrace wall and a grey dog; the name was changed in the editing pass so that no first initial pairs with the author's Egil Hurn), Knut Dahl (Haug's neighbour, named in Area 03 and present in Areas 05 and 06), Hallgerd Vorn (the old woman whose stair has no rope), Ivar Lund (Corporal of the Flightcraft arm, Area 03), Ragna Holm (keeper of the third station, Area 04), Sverre Vik (Airman of the Flightcraft arm, Area 05), and Gudrun Nyland and Leif Bakke (two sentries of the Stone Vigil, Areas 05 and 06). Each first name and surname was scanned as a whole word, case-insensitive, by the Maker over `story-memory`, the skills, the guardrails, the commands, the agents and the written Chapters, with no hit; this lane repeated the scan over the repository outside the archive, agent memory, `.git`, the session log, the temporary folder and the Chapter 005 files, with no hit for any of the nine names. Nyland and Bakke carry a worded level the plan did not give, a Senior of the middle Levels and a Senior of the upper Levels, both the drafting pass's picks inside those bands (the lowest value of each band). The author's seven are unchanged: Sigrun Korth, Torvek Gant, Yrsa Wold, Jorund Ulm, Ingrith Mott, Egil Hurn and Dagmar Sund. The Gate 2 ledger step enters the Tier D rows, with their codes, as the Maker's hand-off lists them. Block 5.0a, the Area 05 garrison row, gains two rows for the sentries (2026-10-19).
- **Areas 03, 05 and 06, the Court's Day 14 hearing (D12)** — Variance (information): Planned: Area 03 has two petitioners "whose small business was not reached before the Court rose" (Tier D by role), and Area 05 has the Court sit and the king "hear a petition in full", with no link between them. Written: the Day 14 hearing is the drain case set in Area 03: the king sets Knut Dahl before the Court "on the morning of Mavil with his drain", and in Area 05 Dahl argues the drain (a spring below Haug's wall; Haug's father's wet corner) while Ottar Haug waits with the grey dog; both stand again in Area 06, Haug's dog pressed flat to the planks and then first down the Wind Crossing. Dahl's speech is reported and never quoted. The second petitioner of Area 03, Hallgerd Vorn, stands in Area 03 only (2026-10-19).

- **Areas 01 to 06, Place, in-story time, weather and light** — Variance: none for the dates. As built, the in-story time of Areas 01 and 02 is Year 1247, Ilovany, Nuvil, Day 9 (Phase Day 9), first light into the morning; of Areas 03 and 04, Year 1247, Ilovany, Alovil, Day 12 (Phase Day 12), the evening into the dark; of Areas 05 and 06, Year 1247, Ilovany, Mavil, Day 14 (Phase Day 14), the morning and, in Area 06, two hours on into the afternoon; the Kind is present throughout. The date lines as written read *Year 1247, Ilovany, Nuvil, Day 9* (once, at the top of the Chapter, below the Area 01 marker, D13), *Year 1247, Ilovany, Alovil, Day 12* (after the break before Area 03) and *Year 1247, Ilovany, Mavil, Day 14* (after the break before Area 05); Areas 02, 04 and 06 print none. The weekdays follow the formula of Part 41 Area 3 with Day 1 an Orvil: Day 9 is Nuvil, Day 12 Alovil and Day 14 Mavil. Dates named inside the text are mentions and not segments: "the morning of Mavil" (Area 03, the king's speech, set for Day 14 and shown in Area 05); "the eighth day of Ulenor" (Area 05, in Varys's letter: Year 1246, Ulenor, Day 8, the back-dated raid of Chapter 003); "the ninth", "the eleventh" and "the fourteenth" (the lieutenant's count and the king's letter); and "Aegon wrote on the eighth" (Area 06, in Aalia's letter: Phase Day 8, the evening on which the letter of Area 03 was written). The weather and light as written: Area 01 first light with mist on the stream and the low road, cold; Area 02 the mist thinning from the top as the light grows; Area 03 an evening easterly, cold, thin and clear, the light going thin and pale; Area 04 dusk into dark with the evening easterly; Area 05 a clear, cold morning with the first warmth of the sun; Area 06 the same easterly, with the sun gone round to the window by the afternoon and the air in her room still until it moves from the east and then the south. The plan's "spring" is not named on the page for any day. The calendar ledger owns the recorded dates (Part 41 Areas 5 and 9); where the spec and the ledger differ, the ledger stands (2026-10-19).
